Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the asteroids framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 502. The resulting operator achieves bound O(49/n^3).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on asteroids structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 502. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 49. QED.

Theorem 219.5 (Spectral Property)

Statement: Under the spectral property assumption on asteroids structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 833. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 38 with relations in degree 5.
